<script>
var Webflow = Webflow || [];

// Version 1.1 - Added data-copies attribute for configurable content duplication

// ─── Global marquee options ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const marqueeSettings = { transitionDuration: 300 };

const mobileStyle = document.createElement('style');
mobileStyle.innerHTML = `
  @media (max-width: 480px) {
    body:not(.is-mobile), body.is-mobile { opacity: 1; }
  }
`;
document.head.appendChild(mobileStyle);

function checkMobile() {
  if (window.innerWidth <= 480) {
    document.body.classList.add('is-mobile');
  } else {
    document.body.classList.remove('is-mobile');
  }
}
window.addEventListener('resize', checkMobile);
checkMobile();

Webflow.push(function () {

  class Marquee {
    constructor(element) {
      if (!element) throw new Error('Marquee element not found');

      this.container = element;
      this.content   = element.children[0];
      if (!this.content) throw new Error('Marquee content not found');

      this.settings = {
        direction:          this.validateDirection(element.getAttribute('data-direction')),
        speed:              this.validateSpeed(element.getAttribute('data-speed')),
        mobileSpeed:        this.validateSpeed(element.getAttribute('data-mobile-speed')),
        hoverSpeed:         this.validateSpeed(element.getAttribute('data-hover-speed')),
        transitionDuration: marqueeSettings.transitionDuration,
        // Read copies from attribute; minimum 2, default 2
        copies:             this.validateCopies(element.getAttribute('data-copies'))
      };

      this.position     = 0;
      this.isMobile     = this.checkIfMobile();
      this.currentSpeed = this.getInitialSpeed();
      this.targetSpeed  = this.currentSpeed;
      this.lastTime     = performance.now();
      this.animation    = null;
      this.wrapper      = null;
      this.isVisible    = true;

      this.animate                = this.animate.bind(this);
      this.setSpeed               = this.setSpeed.bind(this);
      this.handleVisibilityChange = this.handleVisibilityChange.bind(this);
      this.handleResize           = this.handleResize.bind(this);

      this.init();
    }

    /* ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
    checkIfMobile()      { return document.body.classList.contains('is-mobile'); }
    getInitialSpeed()    { return this.isMobile ? this.settings.mobileSpeed : this.settings.speed; }
    validateDirection(d) { return ['left', 'right'].includes(d) ? d : 'left'; }
    validateSpeed(s)     { const v = parseFloat(s); return !isNaN(v) && v >= 0 ? v : 50; }
    lerp(a, b, t)        { return a * (1 - t) + b * t; }

    // Ensures copies is an integer of at least 2
    validateCopies(val) {
      const parsed = parseInt(val, 10);
      return (!isNaN(parsed) && parsed >= 2) ? parsed : 2;
    }

    /* ── Setup ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
    init() {
      this.setupWrapper();
      this.addEventListeners();
      this.lastTime = performance.now();
      this.animate();
    }

    setupWrapper() {
      this.wrapper = document.createElement('div');
      this.wrapper.style.display  = 'flex';
      this.wrapper.style.position = 'relative';

      // Always append the original content first
      this.wrapper.appendChild(this.content);

      // Then append (copies - 1) clones to reach the total requested
      for (let i = 1; i < this.settings.copies; i++) {
        this.wrapper.appendChild(this.content.cloneNode(true));
      }

      this.container.appendChild(this.wrapper);
    }

    addEventListeners() {
      this.container.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => this.setSpeed(this.settings.hoverSpeed));
      this.container.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => this.setSpeed(this.getInitialSpeed()));
      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this.handleVisibilityChange);
      window.addEventListener('resize', this.handleResize);

      window.addEventListener('pagehide', (e) => {
        if (!e.persisted) this.cleanup();
      });
      window.addEventListener('pageshow', () => {
        this.lastTime = performance.now();
        if (!this.animation) this.animate();
      });
    }

    /* ── Event handlers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
    handleResize() {
      const wasMobile = this.isMobile;
      this.isMobile   = this.checkIfMobile();
      if (wasMobile !== this.isMobile) this.setSpeed(this.getInitialSpeed());
    }

    handleVisibilityChange() {
      this.isVisible = document.visibilityState === 'visible';
      if (!this.isVisible) cancelAnimationFrame(this.animation);
      else {
        this.lastTime = performance.now();
        this.animate();
      }
    }

    /* ── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
    setSpeed(speed) {
      this.targetSpeed = this.validateSpeed(speed);
    }

    animate(now = performance.now()) {
      if (!this.isVisible) return;

      const dt          = now - this.lastTime;
      this.lastTime     = now;
      const lerpFactor  = Math.min(dt / this.settings.transitionDuration, 1);
      this.currentSpeed = this.lerp(this.currentSpeed, this.targetSpeed, lerpFactor);
      const dir         = this.settings.direction === 'left' ? -1 : 1;
      this.position    += dir * this.currentSpeed * 0.1;

      const w = this.content.offsetWidth;
      if (Math.abs(this.position) >= w) this.position = 0;
      this.wrapper.style.transform = `translateX(${this.position}px)`;

      this.animation = requestAnimationFrame(this.animate);
    }

    cleanup() {
      cancelAnimationFrame(this.animation);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', this.handleVisibilityChange);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', this.handleResize);
    }
  }

  /* ── Initialise every [data-marquee="true"] element ─────────────────────── */
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-marquee="true"]')
          .forEach(el => new Marquee(el));
});
</script>
<!-- MARQUEE END --></div><div class="tab_timer_code w-embed w-script"><!-- AUTO-TAB TIMER START -->
<script>
// Version 1.1 - Auto-Tab Timer Component
// Tab component with auto-advance timer and animated SVG circle indicator

var Webflow = Webflow || [];
Webflow.push(function () {

  // ─── Constants ──────────────────────────────────────────
  var GROW_MS  = 200;              // Phase 1: dot → ring grow (ms)
  var FADE_MS  = 300;              // Panel fade duration (ms)
  var C        = 2 * Math.PI * 8;  // Ring circumference ≈ 50.27 (r = 8)

  // ─── Find all tab containers ────────────────────────────
  var containers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-tabs-container]');

  if (!containers.length) {
    console.warn('Auto-Tab: No [data-tabs-container] found, exiting.');
    return;
  }

  containers.forEach(function (ctr) {
    try {
      initGroup(ctr);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Auto-Tab: Init error:', err);
    }
  });

  // ─── Init per container ─────────────────────────────────
  function initGroup(ctr) {
    var btns   = Array.from(ctr.querySelectorAll('[data-tab-button]'));
    var panels = Array.from(ctr.querySelectorAll('[data-tab-panel]'));
    var dur    = parseInt(ctr.getAttribute('data-tab-timer'), 10) || 5000;

    if (!btns.length || !panels.length) {
      console.warn('Auto-Tab: Missing buttons or panels in container.');
      return;
    }

    // State
    var activeIdx = 0;
    var stopped   = false;  // permanently stopped after click
    var paused    = false;
    var rafId     = null;
    var elapsed   = 0;
    var lastTs    = null;

    // ── Timer ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    function startTimer() {
      if (stopped) return;
      paused = false;
      lastTs = null;
      rafId  = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function tick(ts) {
      if (paused || stopped) return;
      if (!lastTs) lastTs = ts;

      elapsed += ts - lastTs;
      lastTs   = ts;

      var progress = Math.min(elapsed / dur, 1);
      setProgress(btns[activeIdx], progress);

      if (progress >= 1) {
        goNext();
        return;
      }

      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function pauseTimer() {
      if (stopped) return;
      paused = true;
      lastTs = null;
      if (rafId) { cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
    }

    function resumeTimer() {
      if (stopped || !paused) return;
      paused = false;
      lastTs = null;
      rafId  = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function stopPermanent() {
      stopped = true;
      paused  = true;
      if (rafId) { cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
    }

    function resetTimer() {
      elapsed = 0;
      lastTs  = null;
    }

    // ── Indicator helpers ─────────────────────────────────
    function getSvg(btn)  { return btn.querySelector('[data-tab-indicator="true"]'); }
    function getDot(btn)  { var s = getSvg(btn); return s ? s.querySelector('[data-indicator-dot]')  : null; }
    function getRing(btn) { var s = getSvg(btn); return s ? s.querySelector('[data-indicator-ring]') : null; }

    function setProgress(btn, progress) {
      var r = getRing(btn);
      if (r) r.style.strokeDashoffset = String(C * progress);
    }

    function initIndicator(btn) {
      var s = getSvg(btn), d = getDot(btn), r = getRing(btn);
      if (!s) return;

      // Clockwise from 12 o'clock
      s.style.transform       = 'rotate(-90deg)';
      s.style.transformOrigin  = 'center';
      s.style.overflow         = 'visible';

      if (d) {
        d.style.transition = 'none';
        d.style.opacity    = '1';
      }

      if (r) {
        r.style.strokeDasharray  = String(C);
        r.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';
        r.style.opacity          = '0';
        r.style.transformBox     = 'fill-box';
        r.style.transformOrigin  = 'center';
        r.style.transform        = 'scale(0.375)';
        r.style.transition       = 'none';
      }
    }

    // Phase 1: dot fades out → ring scales up + fades in
    function activateIndicator(btn, cb) {
      var d = getDot(btn), r = getRing(btn);
      if (!d || !r) { if (cb) cb(); return; }

      // Reset ring to full circle
      r.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';

      // Use rAF so transition triggers after initial style reset
      requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        d.style.transition = 'opacity ' + GROW_MS + 'ms ease-out';
        d.style.opacity    = '0';

        r.style.transition = 'opacity ' + GROW_MS + 'ms ease-out, transform ' + GROW_MS + 'ms ease-out';
        r.style.opacity    = '1';
        r.style.transform  = 'scale(1)';
      });

      setTimeout(function () { if (cb) cb(); }, GROW_MS);
    }

    // Reverse: ring shrinks + fades out → dot fades in
    function deactivateIndicator(btn) {
      var d = getDot(btn), r = getRing(btn);
      if (!d || !r) return;

      d.style.transition = 'opacity ' + GROW_MS + 'ms ease-in';
      d.style.opacity    = '1';

      r.style.transition = 'opacity ' + GROW_MS + 'ms ease-in, transform ' + GROW_MS + 'ms ease-in';
      r.style.opacity    = '0';
      r.style.transform  = 'scale(0.375)';
    }

    // ── Tab switching ─────────────────────────────────────
    function switchTab(newIdx, isClick) {
      if (isClick) {
        stopPermanent();
      } else {
        if (rafId) { cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
      }

      var oldIdx = activeIdx;
      activeIdx  = newIdx;

      // Update ARIA & combo class
      btns.forEach(function (btn, i) {
        var sel = i === newIdx;
        btn.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(sel));
        btn.setAttribute('tabindex', sel ? '0' : '-1');

        if (sel) {
          btn.classList.add('is-active');
        } else {
          btn.classList.remove('is-active');
        }
      });

      // Deactivate old indicator
      if (oldIdx !== newIdx) {
        deactivateIndicator(btns[oldIdx]);
      }

      // Fade panels
      panels.forEach(function (panel) {
        var tabId = btns[newIdx].getAttribute('data-tab-button');
        var match = panel.getAttribute('data-tab-panel') === tabId;

        if (match) {
          panel.hidden  = false;
          panel.style.opacity    = '0';
          panel.style.transition = 'opacity ' + FADE_MS + 'ms ease-in';
          requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            panel.style.opacity = '1';
          });
        } else {
          panel.style.transition = 'opacity ' + FADE_MS + 'ms ease-in';
          panel.style.opacity    = '0';
          var p = panel;
          setTimeout(function () { p.hidden = true; }, FADE_MS);
        }
      });

      // Activate new indicator → start timer
      resetTimer();
      activateIndicator(btns[newIdx], function () {
        if (!stopped && !paused) {
          startTimer();
        }
      });
    }

    function goNext() {
      switchTab((activeIdx + 1) % btns.length, false);
    }

    // ── Events ────────────────────────────────────────────

    // Click
    btns.forEach(function (btn, i) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        switchTab(i, true);
      });

      // Keyboard navigation
      btn.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
        var ci = btns.indexOf(e.target);
        var ni;

        switch (e.key) {
          case 'ArrowLeft':
          case 'ArrowUp':
            ni = (ci - 1 + btns.length) % btns.length;
            break;
          case 'ArrowRight':
          case 'ArrowDown':
            ni = (ci + 1) % btns.length;
            break;
          case 'Home':
            ni = 0;
            break;
          case 'End':
            ni = btns.length - 1;
            break;
          default:
            return;
        }

        btns[ni].focus();
        switchTab(ni, true);
        e.preventDefault();
      });
    });

    // Hover pause / resume (tablist + panels only)
    var tablist = ctr.querySelector('[role="tablist"]');
    var hoverCount = 0;

    function onHoverIn() {
      hoverCount++;
      if (hoverCount === 1) pauseTimer();
    }

    function onHoverOut() {
      hoverCount--;
      if (hoverCount <= 0) {
        hoverCount = 0;
        resumeTimer();
      }
    }

    if (tablist) {
      tablist.addEventListener('mouseenter', onHoverIn);
      tablist.addEventListener('mouseleave', onHoverOut);
    }

    panels.forEach(function (panel) {
      panel.addEventListener('mouseenter', onHoverIn);
      panel.addEventListener('mouseleave', onHoverOut);
    });

    // Focus pause / resume (tablist + panels)
    var focusCount = 0;

    function onFocusIn() {
      focusCount++;
      if (focusCount === 1) pauseTimer();
    }

    function onFocusOut(e) {
      focusCount--;
      if (focusCount <= 0) {
        focusCount = 0;
        var target = e.relatedTarget;
        if (!tablist || !tablist.contains(target)) {
          var inPanel = panels.some(function (p) { return p.contains(target); });
          if (!inPanel) resumeTimer();
        }
      }
    }

    if (tablist) {
      tablist.addEventListener('focusin', onFocusIn);
      tablist.addEventListener('focusout', onFocusOut);
    }

    panels.forEach(function (panel) {
      panel.addEventListener('focusin', onFocusIn);
      panel.addEventListener('focusout', onFocusOut);
    });

    // ── Initialize ────────────────────────────────────────
    btns.forEach(initIndicator);
    switchTab(0, false);
  }
});
</script>
<!-- AUTO-TAB TIMER END --></div><div class="w-embed w-script"><!-- Slider Navigation Script START -->
<script>
// Version 2.16.1 - Stop Offset: Configurable End-Boundary Inset (rem)

var Webflow = Webflow || [];
Webflow.push(function () {

  const sliderConfig = {
    enableDesktopDrag: true,
    enableMobileDrag: true,
    dragSensitivity: 1.2,
    transitionDuration: 300,
    transitionTimingFunction: 'cubic-bezier(0.37, 0, 0.63, 1)',
    startSlide: 0,
    mobileBreakpoint: 768
  };

  /**
   * Determine if current viewport is mobile
   * @returns {boolean}
   */
  const isMobile = () => window.innerWidth < sliderConfig.mobileBreakpoint;

  /**
   * Convert rem value to pixels
   * @param {number} rem - Value in rem units
   * @returns {number} Value in pixels
   */
  const remToPx = (rem) => {
    return rem * parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
  };

  // Ensure DOM is ready before initializing
  function initSliders() {
    const sliderWrappers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-slider-wrapper="true"]');

    if (sliderWrappers.length === 0) {
      console.warn('No slider wrappers found with [data-slider-wrapper="true"]');
      return;
    }

    sliderWrappers.forEach(initializeSlider);
  }

  /**
   * Initialize individual slider with accessibility features
   * @param {HTMLElement} sliderWrapper - The slider wrapper element
   */
  function initializeSlider(sliderWrapper) {
    // Validate slider wrapper exists
    if (!sliderWrapper) {
      console.error('Slider wrapper element is null or undefined');
      return;
    }

    /* ----- DOM References ----- */
    const sliderTrack = sliderWrapper.querySelector('[data-slider-track="true"]');
    if (!sliderTrack) {
      console.error('Slider track not found for wrapper:', sliderWrapper);
      return;
    }

    const slides = Array.from(sliderTrack.children);
    if (slides.length === 0) {
      console.error('No slides found in slider track');
      return;
    }

    const sliderId = sliderWrapper.getAttribute('data-slider-id') || 'default';

    // Get all navigation buttons (prev/next arrows)
    const sliderBtns = document.querySelectorAll(
      `[data-slider][data-slider-id="${sliderId}"]`
    );

    // Separate prev and next buttons for boundary class management
    const prevBtns = document.querySelectorAll(
      `[data-slider="prev"][data-slider-id="${sliderId}"]`
    );
    const nextBtns = document.querySelectorAll(
      `[data-slider="next"][data-slider-id="${sliderId}"]`
    );

    // Get navigation dots wrapper
    const dotsWrapper = document.querySelector(
      `[data-slider-nav="true"][data-slider-id="${sliderId}"]`
    );

    const navItems = dotsWrapper
      ? Array.from(dotsWrapper.querySelectorAll('button'))
      : [];

    if (navItems.length === 0 && dotsWrapper) {
      console.warn('Navigation dots wrapper found but no buttons inside');
    }

    /**
     * Helper function to safely read data attributes
     * @param {string} attr - Attribute name (without 'data-' prefix)
     * @param {*} def - Default value if attribute not found
     * @param {Function} cast - Optional casting function
     * @returns {*} Parsed attribute value or default
     */
    const getDataAttr = (attr, def, cast = v => v) => {
      try {
        const raw = sliderWrapper.getAttribute(`data-${attr}`);
        return raw !== null ? cast(raw) : def;
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn(`Error reading data-${attr}:`, error);
        return def;
      }
    };

    /* ----- Per-Slider Configuration ----- */
    const enableDesktopDrag = getDataAttr('desktop-drag', sliderConfig.enableDesktopDrag, v => v.toLowerCase() === 'true');
    const enableMobileDrag = getDataAttr('mobile-drag', sliderConfig.enableMobileDrag, v => v.toLowerCase() === 'true');
    const alignment = getDataAttr('align', 'center', v => v.toLowerCase());
    const customStartSlide = getDataAttr('start-slide', sliderConfig.startSlide, parseInt);
    const dragSensitivity = getDataAttr('drag-sensitivity', sliderConfig.dragSensitivity, parseFloat);
    const transitionDuration = getDataAttr('transition-duration', sliderConfig.transitionDuration, parseInt);
    const regionLabel = getDataAttr('region-label', 'Carousel');
    const loopDesktop = getDataAttr('loop-desktop', true, v => v.toLowerCase() === 'true');
    const loopMobile = getDataAttr('loop-mobile', true, v => v.toLowerCase() === 'true');
    const stopOffsetRem = getDataAttr('stop-offset', 0, parseFloat);

    /**
     * Check if looping is enabled for the current device
     * @returns {boolean}
     */
    const isLoopEnabled = () => isMobile() ? loopMobile : loopDesktop;

    /* ----- Accessibility Setup ----- */
    sliderWrapper.setAttribute('role', 'region');
    sliderWrapper.setAttribute('aria-label', `${regionLabel}, ${slides.length} slides`);

    /* ----- State Management ----- */
    let currentIndex = Math.min(Math.max(customStartSlide, 0), slides.length - 1);
    let startPos = 0;
    let currentTranslate = 0;
    let prevTranslate = 0;
    let isDragging = false;
    let animationID = 0;

    /**
     * Get the width of a single slide
     * @returns {number} Width in pixels
     */
    const getSlideW = () => {
      const firstSlide = slides[0];
      if (!firstSlide) return 0;
      return firstSlide.getBoundingClientRect().width;
    };

    /**
     * Get computed style value as number
     * @param {HTMLElement} el - Element
     * @param {string} prop - CSS property name
     * @returns {number} Property value in pixels
     */
    const getStyleVal = (el, prop) => {
      try {
        return parseFloat(getComputedStyle(el).getPropertyValue(prop));
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn(`Error reading style property ${prop}:`, error);
        return 0;
      }
    };

    /**
     * Get the gap between slides (recalculated for responsiveness)
     * @returns {number} Gap in pixels
     */
    const getSlideGap = () => {
      if (slides.length === 0) return 0;
      return getStyleVal(slides[0], 'margin-right');
    };

    /**
     * Calculate total slide width including gap
     * @returns {number} Total width in pixels
     */
    const slideTotal = () => getSlideW() + getSlideGap();

    /**
     * Calculate the minimum translate value (furthest left position)
     * Used for left-aligned clamping so the last slide's right edge
     * aligns with the wrapper's right edge, minus any stop offset.
     * @returns {number} Minimum translate value (negative number)
     */
    const getMinTranslate = () => {
      const totalSlidesWidth = (slides.length * slideTotal()) - getSlideGap();
      const wrapperWidth = sliderWrapper.offsetWidth;
      const offsetPx = remToPx(stopOffsetRem);
      return Math.min(0, -(totalSlidesWidth - wrapperWidth) + offsetPx);
    };

    /**
     * Calculate the maximum translate value (furthest right position)
     * @returns {number} Maximum translate value (0 for left-aligned)
     */
    const getMaxTranslate = () => {
      if (alignment === 'center') {
        return (sliderWrapper.offsetWidth - getSlideW()) / 2;
      }
      return 0;
    };

    /**
     * Clamp a translate value within allowed boundaries
     * Only applies when alignment is "left" and loop is disabled
     * @param {number} translate - The proposed translate value
     * @returns {number} Clamped translate value
     */
    const clampTranslate = (translate) => {
      if (alignment !== 'left' || isLoopEnabled()) {
        return translate;
      }
      const min = getMinTranslate();
      const max = getMaxTranslate();
      return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, translate));
    };

    /**
     * Calculate position for a given slide index
     * @param {number} idx - Slide index
     * @returns {number} Translate position in pixels
     */
    const calcPos = (idx) => {
      const offset = alignment === 'center'
        ? (sliderWrapper.offsetWidth - getSlideW()) / 2
        : 0;
      const rawTranslate = -(idx * slideTotal()) + offset;
      return clampTranslate(rawTranslate);
    };

    /**
     * Apply transform to slider track
     */
    const setPos = () => {
      sliderTrack.style.transition = isDragging
        ? 'none'
        : `transform ${transitionDuration}ms ${sliderConfig.transitionTimingFunction}`;
      sliderTrack.style.transform = `translateX(${currentTranslate}px)`;
    };

    /**
     * Update boundary combo classes on navigation buttons
     * Adds cc-button-start to prev buttons at start boundary
     * Adds cc-button-end to next buttons at end boundary
     * Only applied when loop is disabled for current device
     */
    const updateBoundaryClasses = () => {
      const loop = isLoopEnabled();
      let atStart = false;
      let atEnd = false;

      if (!loop) {
        if (alignment === 'center') {
          atStart = currentIndex === 0;
          atEnd = currentIndex === slides.length - 1;
        } else {
          atStart = currentTranslate >= getMaxTranslate();
          atEnd = currentTranslate <= getMinTranslate();
        }
      }

      // Update prev buttons
      prevBtns.forEach(btn => {
        if (atStart) {
          btn.classList.add('cc-button-start');
        } else {
          btn.classList.remove('cc-button-start');
        }
      });

      // Update next buttons
      nextBtns.forEach(btn => {
        if (atEnd) {
          btn.classList.add('cc-button-end');
        } else {
          btn.classList.remove('cc-button-end');
        }
      });
    };

    /**
     * Update ARIA attributes for current slide
     */
    const updateAriaStates = () => {
      slides.forEach((s, i) => {
        s.setAttribute('aria-hidden', i === currentIndex ? 'false' : 'true');
      });

      navItems.forEach((n, i) => {
        n.setAttribute('aria-current', i === currentIndex ? 'true' : 'false');
      });
    };

    /**
     * Navigate to specific slide index
     * @param {number} idx - Slide index to navigate to
     */
    const goTo = (idx) => {
      if (idx < 0 || idx >= slides.length) {
        console.warn(`Slide index ${idx} out of range [0, ${slides.length - 1}]`);
        return;
      }

      currentIndex = idx;
      currentTranslate = calcPos(idx);
      prevTranslate = currentTranslate;
      setPos();

      // Update active slide content via attribute selector
      slides.forEach((s, i) => {
        const slideContent = s.querySelector('[data-slide-content="true"]');
        if (slideContent) {
          slideContent.classList.toggle('slide-inactive', i !== idx);
        }
      });

      // Update navigation dots
      navItems.forEach((n, i) => {
        n.classList.toggle('active', i === idx);
      });

      updateAriaStates();
      updateBoundaryClasses();
    };

    /**
     * Move to adjacent slide with loop/boundary awareness
     * @param {number} dir - Direction: -1 for previous, 1 for next
     */
    const move = (dir) => {
      const nextIndex = currentIndex + dir;
      const loop = isLoopEnabled();

      if (nextIndex < 0) {
        if (loop) {
          goTo(slides.length - 1);
        }
        return;
      }

      if (nextIndex >= slides.length) {
        if (loop) {
          goTo(0);
        }
        return;
      }

      // For left-aligned non-looping: prevent advancing past clamped end
      if (alignment === 'left' && !loop && dir === 1) {
        const proposedTranslate = calcPos(nextIndex);
        const minTranslate = getMinTranslate();
        if (currentTranslate <= minTranslate && proposedTranslate <= minTranslate) {
          return;
        }
      }

      goTo(nextIndex);
    };

    /**
     * Extract X coordinate from mouse or touch event
     * @param {Event} e - Mouse or touch event
     * @returns {number} X coordinate
     */
    const getX = (e) => {
      if (e.type.includes('mouse')) {
        return e.pageX;
      }
      if (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) {
        return e.touches[0].clientX;
      }
      return 0;
    };

    /* ----- Drag Handlers ----- */

    const dragStart = (e) => {
      if ((!enableDesktopDrag && e.type.includes('mouse')) ||
        (!enableMobileDrag && e.type.includes('touch'))) {
        return;
      }

      startPos = getX(e);
      isDragging = true;
      sliderTrack.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
      sliderTrack.style.transition = 'none';
      cancelAnimationFrame(animationID);
    };

    const dragMove = (e) => {
      if (!isDragging) return;

      const currentPosition = getX(e);
      if (currentPosition === 0) return;

      let rawTranslate = prevTranslate + (currentPosition - startPos) * dragSensitivity;
      currentTranslate = clampTranslate(rawTranslate);
    };

    const dragEnd = () => {
      if (!isDragging) return;
      isDragging = false;
      sliderTrack.style.cursor = 'grab';

      const movedBy = currentTranslate - prevTranslate;
      const threshold = slideTotal() / 4;

      if (movedBy < -threshold) {
        move(1);
      } else if (movedBy > threshold) {
        move(-1);
      } else {
        goTo(currentIndex);
      }
    };

    const animate = () => {
      setPos();
      if (isDragging) {
        animationID = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }
    };

    /* ----- Keyboard Navigation ----- */

    const handleKeydown = (e) => {
      if (!sliderWrapper.contains(document.activeElement)) {
        return;
      }

      const keyMap = {
        'ArrowLeft': () => move(-1),
        'ArrowRight': () => move(1),
        'Home': () => goTo(0),
        'End': () => goTo(slides.length - 1)
      };

      if (keyMap[e.key]) {
        e.preventDefault();
        keyMap[e.key]();
      }
    };

    /* ----- Event Listeners ----- */

    sliderBtns.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const direction = btn.getAttribute('data-slider') === 'prev' ? -1 : 1;
        move(direction);
      });
    });

    navItems.forEach((item, i) => {
      item.setAttribute('aria-label', `Slide ${i + 1}`);
      item.setAttribute('role', 'button');

      item.addEventListener('click', () => {
        goTo(i);
      });
    });

    sliderTrack.addEventListener('mousedown', dragStart);
    sliderTrack.addEventListener('touchstart', dragStart, { passive: true });
    sliderTrack.addEventListener('mousemove', dragMove);
    sliderTrack.addEventListener('touchmove', dragMove, { passive: true });
    sliderTrack.addEventListener('mouseup', dragEnd);
    sliderTrack.addEventListener('mouseleave', dragEnd);
    sliderTrack.addEventListener('touchend', dragEnd);

    ['mousedown', 'touchstart'].forEach(evt => {
      sliderTrack.addEventListener(evt, () => {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animationID);
        animate();
      });
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown);

    const handleResize = () => {
      goTo(currentIndex);
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);

    /* ----- Initialize Slider ----- */
    goTo(currentIndex);
  }

  // Initialize when DOM is ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSliders);
  } else {
    initSliders();
  }
});
</script>

<script>
// Version 1.1 - Swipe Snap Component
var Webflow = Webflow || [];
Webflow.push(function () {
  var DEFAULTS = {
    align: 'start',
    offset: '0rem',
    breakpoint: null,
    hideScrollbar: false
  };

  var containers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-swipe-snap="true"]');

  if (!containers.length) {
    console.warn('Swipe Snap: No containers found with [data-swipe-snap="true"]');
    return;
  }

  // Inject scrollbar-hiding styles once if any container needs it
  var scrollbarStyleInjected = false;
  function injectScrollbarStyle() {
    if (scrollbarStyleInjected) return;
    var style = document.createElement('style');
    style.textContent =
      '[data-swipe-snap][data-snap-hide-scrollbar="true"] {' +
      '  scrollbar-width: none;' +
      '  -ms-overflow-style: none;' +
      '}' +
      '[data-swipe-snap][data-snap-hide-scrollbar="true"]::-webkit-scrollbar {' +
      '  display: none;' +
      '}';
    document.head.appendChild(style);
    scrollbarStyleInjected = true;
  }

  function applySnap(container) {
    var align = container.getAttribute('data-snap-align') || DEFAULTS.align;
    var offset = container.getAttribute('data-snap-offset') || DEFAULTS.offset;
    var breakpoint = container.getAttribute('data-snap-breakpoint') || DEFAULTS.breakpoint;
    var hideScrollbar = container.getAttribute('data-snap-hide-scrollbar') === 'true';

    if (hideScrollbar) {
      injectScrollbarStyle();
    }

    function enable() {
      container.style.overflowX = 'auto';
      container.style.scrollSnapType = 'x mandatory';
      container.style.scrollPaddingInlineStart = offset;
      container.style.webkitOverflowScrolling = 'touch';

      var children = container.children;
      for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
        children[i].style.scrollSnapAlign = align;
      }
    }

    function disable() {
      container.style.overflowX = '';
      container.style.scrollSnapType = '';
      container.style.scrollPaddingInlineStart = '';
      container.style.webkitOverflowScrolling = '';

      var children = container.children;
      for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
        children[i].style.scrollSnapAlign = '';
      }
    }

    if (breakpoint) {
      var mq = window.matchMedia('(max-width: ' + breakpoint + ')');

      function handleBreakpoint(e) {
        if (e.matches) {
          enable();
        } else {
          disable();
        }
      }

      // Initial check
      handleBreakpoint(mq);

      // Listen for changes
      if (mq.addEventListener) {
        mq.addEventListener('change', handleBreakpoint);
      } else {
        mq.addListener(handleBreakpoint);
      }
    } else {
      // No breakpoint — always active
      enable();
    }
  }

  try {
    containers.forEach(function (container) {
      applySnap(container);
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Swipe Snap: Error initializing component:', error);
  }
});
</script>

<script>
  //Lock body scroll when nav is open
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', (event) => {
    // Function to handle scroll lock based on menu state
    function updateScrollLock() {
      if ($('.nav-btn').hasClass('w--open')) {
        // Lock the main page scroll but allow scrolling in the menu
        $('body').css({
          overflow: 'hidden',
          position: 'fixed',
          width: '100%',
        });

        // Enable scrolling within the nav menu
        $('.nav-menu').css({
          'overflow-y': 'auto',
          'max-height': '100vh', // viewport height
          '-webkit-overflow-scrolling': 'touch', // for smoother scrolling on iOS
        });
      } else {
        // Reset all scroll-related styles when menu is closed
        $('body').css({
          overflow: 'auto',
          position: 'static',
          width: 'auto',
        });

        $('.nav-menu').css({
          'overflow-y': '',
          'max-height': '',
          '-webkit-overflow-scrolling': '',
        });
      }
    }

    // Handle initial nav button clicks
    $('.nav-btn, .nav-overlay').click(function () {
      setTimeout(updateScrollLock, 100);
    });

    // Create a mutation observer to watch for class changes on nav button
    const observer = new MutationObserver((mutations) => {
      mutations.forEach((mutation) => {
        if (mutation.attributeName === 'class') {
          updateScrollLock();
        }
      });
    });

    // Start observing the nav button for class changes
    const navBtn = document.querySelector('.nav-btn');
    observer.observe(navBtn, { attributes: true });
  });

  // Skip nav accessibility function
  $(function () {
    $('#skip-nav').on('click keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.type === 'keydown' && e.which !== 13) {
        return;
      }

      e.preventDefault();
      var target = $('#main');
      target.attr('tabindex', '-1');
      target.focus();
    });
  });

  $(function () {
    // Set Footer Copyright Year by ID
    $('#copyright-year').text(new Date().getFullYear());
  });
  // Form submit success animation trigger
  $('.form').submit(() => {
    setTimeout(() => {
      $('.form-success_trigger').click();
    }, 1000);
  });
</script>

<script>
  let hsApiUrl = 'https://api.hsforms.com/submissions/v3/integration/submit/24202679/';
  let formData = {
    fields: [],
  };
  let UTMs = ['utm_medium', 'utm_source', 'utm_campaign', 'utm_term', 'utm_content'];

  $('[hs-form]').on('submit', function (event) {
    let hsForm = $(this);
    let hsFormID = $(this).attr('hs-form');
    if (hsFormID == undefined) return;
    let hsEndPoint = hsApiUrl + hsFormID;
    let redirectURL = $(this).attr('redirect-url');
    // Add the form data to the formData object
    hsForm.find('[hs-form-field]').each(function () {
      let field = {
        name: $(this).attr('hs-form-field'),
        value: $(this).val(),
      };
      formData.fields.push(field);
    });
    // Add the UTM parameters to the formData object
    UTMs.forEach((utm) => {
      let utmValue = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(utm);
      if (utmValue != null) {
        let field = {
          name: `${utm}`,
          value: utmValue,
        };
        formData.fields.push(field);
      }
    });
    console.log('HubSpot Form Data:', formData);
    // Get page information
    let pageName = document.title; // Use the page title as the name
    let pageUrl = window.location.href; // Get the full URL of the page
    let hubspotCookie = document.cookie.match(/(?:^|;\s*)hubspotutk\s*=\s*([^;]*)/);
    let htuk = hubspotCookie ? hubspotCookie[1] : null;
    // Create the hs_context object
    if (htuk) {
      formData.context = {
        hutk: htuk,
        pageUri: pageUrl,
        pageName: pageName,
      };
    }
    // Make a POST request to HubSpot
    fetch(hsEndPoint, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(formData),
    })
      .then((response) => response.json())
      .then((data) => {
        console.log('HubSpot Form Submission Successful:', data);

        const { redirectUri } = data;

        if (redirectURL != undefined) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            window.location = redirectURL;
          }, 1000);
        } else if (redirectUri != undefined) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            window.location = redirectUri;
          }, 1000);
        }
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        console.error('HubSpot Form Submission Error:', error);
        // Handle error, e.g., show an error message to the user
      });
  });
</script>

<script>
// Webflow Image Opacity Controller
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Only run on screens 992px and above
    if (window.innerWidth < 992) {
        return;
    }
    // Get all trigger divs with the data-trigger attribute
    const triggers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-trigger]');
    
    // Get all portrait images
    const images = document.querySelectorAll('.hps_image');
    
    // Function to set image opacities
    function setImageOpacities(activeImageNumbers) {
        images.forEach((image, index) => {
            const imageNumber = index + 1; // Images are 1-indexed (_01, _02, etc.)
            
            if (activeImageNumbers.includes(imageNumber)) {
                image.style.opacity = '1'; // 100% opacity
            } else {
                image.style.opacity = '0.1'; // 10% opacity
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Set default state on page load
    const defaultTrigger = document.querySelector('[data-trigger][data-default]');
    if (defaultTrigger) {
        const defaultImages = defaultTrigger.getAttribute('data-images');
        if (defaultImages) {
            const activeImages = defaultImages.split(',').map(num => parseInt(num.trim()));
            setImageOpacities(activeImages);
        }
    }
    
    // Add hover event listeners to each trigger
    triggers.forEach(trigger => {
        trigger.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
            // Get the data-images attribute and parse it
            const imageNumbers = this.getAttribute('data-images');
            if (imageNumbers) {
                // Convert string "1,5,12" to array of numbers [1, 5, 12]
                const activeImages = imageNumbers.split(',').map(num => parseInt(num.trim()));
                setImageOpacities(activeImages);
            }
        });
    });

    // COLLAPSE FUNCTIONALITY
    // Get all collapse triggers and content elements (looking for the attribute regardless of value)
    const collapseTriggers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-collapse-trigger]');
    const collapseContents = document.querySelectorAll('[data-collapse-content]');
    
    // Add CSS properties to all collapse content elements
    collapseContents.forEach(content => {
        content.style.transition = 'max-height 250ms ease-in-out';
        content.style.overflow = 'hidden'; // Essential for smooth animation
    });
    
    // Add CSS transition to all collapse trigger buttons
    collapseTriggers.forEach(trigger => {
        trigger.style.transition = 'opacity 250ms ease-in-out';
    });
    
    // Function to collapse all elements except the active one
    function setCollapseStates(activeContent) {
        collapseContents.forEach(content => {
            if (content === activeContent) {
                // Expand: set to scroll height in pixels for smooth animation
                content.style.maxHeight = `${content.scrollHeight}px`;
            } else {
                // Collapse: set to 0px
                content.style.maxHeight = '0px';
            }
        });
        
        // Update button opacities
        collapseTriggers.forEach(trigger => {
            const siblingContent = trigger.parentElement.querySelector('[data-collapse-content]');
            if (siblingContent === activeContent) {
                trigger.style.opacity = '1';
            } else {
                trigger.style.opacity = '0.5';
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Set initial state - find the active collapse content and set it properly
    const activeContent = document.querySelector('[data-collapse-content].is-active');
    if (activeContent) {
        // First set all to no transition temporarily to avoid initial animation
        collapseContents.forEach(content => {
            content.style.transition = 'none';
            content.style.maxHeight = '0px';
        });
        collapseTriggers.forEach(trigger => {
            trigger.style.transition = 'none';
            trigger.style.opacity = '0.5';
        });
        
        // Force reflow
        activeContent.offsetHeight;
        
        // Re-enable transitions
        collapseContents.forEach(content => {
            content.style.transition = 'max-height 250ms ease-in-out';
        });
        collapseTriggers.forEach(trigger => {
            trigger.style.transition = 'opacity 250ms ease-in-out';
        });
        
        // Now set the active state properly
        activeContent.style.maxHeight = `${activeContent.scrollHeight}px`;
        const activeButton = activeContent.parentElement.querySelector('[data-collapse-trigger]');
        if (activeButton) {
            activeButton.style.opacity = '1';
        }
    } else {
        // If no active class, collapse all and set all buttons to 50% opacity
        collapseContents.forEach(content => {
            content.style.maxHeight = '0px';
        });
        collapseTriggers.forEach(trigger => {
            trigger.style.opacity = '0.5';
        });
    }
    
    // Add hover event listeners to collapse triggers
    collapseTriggers.forEach(trigger => {
        trigger.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
            // Find the sibling collapse content
            const siblingContent = this.parentElement.querySelector('[data-collapse-content]');
            if (siblingContent) {
                setCollapseStates(siblingContent);
            }
        });
    });
});
</script>
<!-- PLATFORM UP NEXT - END -->

<!-- IMAGES FADE IN START -->
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  // Get elements
  const track = document.querySelector('[data-scroll-track="true"]');
  const wrappers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-image-wrapper="true"]');
  
  if (!track || !wrappers.length) return;
  
  // Initialize each wrapper
  wrappers.forEach(wrapper => {
    const images = Array.from(wrapper.querySelectorAll('[data-fade-image="true"]'));
    if (images.length === 0) return;
    
    // Create a random order for the images
    const shuffledIndices = images.map((_, i) => i)
      .sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    
    // Set initial state for all images
    images.forEach(img => {
      img.style.opacity = 0;
      img.style.transition = 'opacity 0.1s ease-out';
    });

    // Calculate the range for each image
    const ranges = shuffledIndices.map((_, index) => {
      const totalImages = images.length;
      return {
        start: (index / totalImages) * 100,
        end: ((index + 1) / totalImages) * 100
      };
    });

    function updateImages(progress) {
      shuffledIndices.forEach((originalIndex, shuffledPosition) => {
        const image = images[originalIndex];
        const range = ranges[shuffledPosition];
        
        let opacity;
        if (progress >= range.end) {
          opacity = 1;
        } else if (progress <= range.start) {
          opacity = 0;
        } else {
          opacity = (progress - range.start) / (range.end - range.start);
        }
        
        image.style.opacity = opacity;
      });
    }

    // Store the update function for this wrapper
    wrapper.updateImages = updateImages;
  });

  function getScrollProgress() {
    const rect = track.getBoundingClientRect();
    const windowHeight = window.innerHeight;
    
    // Get offset values from attributes (default to 20 and 50 if not set)
    const startOffset = parseFloat(track.getAttribute('data-start-percent') || 20) / 100;
    const endOffset = parseFloat(track.getAttribute('data-end-percent') || 50) / 100;
    
    // Calculate total scrollable distance and how far we've scrolled
    const totalDistance = rect.height + windowHeight;
    const currentPoint = windowHeight - rect.top;
    
    // Adjust the progress range based on offsets
    const effectiveStart = totalDistance * startOffset;
    const effectiveEnd = totalDistance * (1 - endOffset);
    
    // Calculate progress within the adjusted range
    const adjustedProgress = ((currentPoint - effectiveStart) / (effectiveEnd - effectiveStart)) * 100;
    
    // Clamp between 0 and 100
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, adjustedProgress));
  }

  // Optimize scroll handler with requestAnimationFrame
  let ticking = false;
  
  document.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
    if (!ticking) {
      window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const progress = getScrollProgress();
        // Update all wrappers with the current progress
        wrappers.forEach(wrapper => {
          if (wrapper.updateImages) {
            wrapper.updateImages(progress);
          }
        });
        ticking = false;
      });
      ticking = true;
    }
  });


  // Initial update
  const initialProgress = getScrollProgress();
  wrappers.forEach(wrapper => {
    if (wrapper.updateImages) {
      wrapper.updateImages(initialProgress);
    }
  });
});
</script>
